# STANDING_VIEW_US — the US desk's per-name carry

> ★ **Split from `STANDING_VIEW.md` on 2026-07-29** (the 8-run escalation, resolved by a human).
> **This file is the `industry_US` desk's half. The SHARED SPINE — the regime call, the seed
> measured chain, §4's asymmetry, §5's retracted ledger and §6's open contradictions — stays in
> [`STANDING_VIEW.md`](STANDING_VIEW.md) and is read IN FULL by both desks every run.**
>
> **Reading rule.** A US run reads `STANDING_VIEW.md` + this file in full, and reads
> `STANDING_VIEW_KR.md` **only when it touches a KR name** (it did on 2026-07-29, to score S28/S33).
> **The split reduces default read cost; it does not wall the desks off** — nothing here may be
> cited without the spine's §5 having been read first.
>
> Every claim is tagged `[measured]` or `[inferred]`. **An `[inferred]` claim may be carried; it may
> not be cited as evidence.** Not advice.

The sweep scored **299/299** after two `scored=0` runs, and `module_chart` recovered to **3/3**. ⇒ the 08-30/08-31 framing ("a rolling T-1 hole") is superseded: it is a **fixed hole at one date** | `[measured]` |

# §2 fact rows — appended by `industry_US`, 2026-09-02 (Wed) · **`M1234` – `M1261`**

> ⚠ Append-only. Every row carries `[measured]` / `[inferred]`; **no `[inferred]` row may be cited as
> evidence downstream.** All flow numbers are from `SECTOR_FLOW_US_REPAIRED.json`, 3-axis `nonews`,
> **asof 2026-09-01**, and every Δ is a **ONE-session (08-31 → 09-01)** quantity used as a
> **direction, not a magnitude** (the baseline is the previous run's holed-OBV primary snapshot).
> Benchmark **`SPY`** named inline (`C1`).

## 2a · Instrument facts

| id | fact | tag |
|---|---|---|
